### Batch D1-F9: Staged Execution Kernel Cutover Follow-ups

目标：
- 将外部 prompt 里仍成立的 staged-execution gap 转成可执行 cutover 批次，优先修真正影响架构稳定性的控制面 gap。
- 保持现有稳定 stage shell、BaseAgent ReAct loop、controller-first write workflow、typed criteria 和 EvidenceClosure reducer family；不引入大 GraphExecutor，不用 prompt/用户关键词/模型散文作 hard route。

任务顺序：
1. **D1-F9a hard-dependency readiness authority**：为 TaskGraph 建 typed predecessor-readiness projection，先加测试证明当前 explore window 对 hard_dependency 的行为，再让 ready-window 合并 `NodeExecStatus`、hard_dependency、`EntryConditions` 三类 precise signal。Optional/soft edges 保持非 hard block。Status: complete.
   - Original code fact at `f21a56e0a`/`1d64b1f07`: `scheduler.go` header said hard-dependency predecessors must be `nodeDone`, but `readyExplorerWindowContext` checked only node status plus `EntryConditions`; `TestGraphState_ReadyWindow_MergedInitial` explicitly expected the old merged chain window.
   - Implementation tasks:
     1. Add a typed helper such as `hardDependencyBlockersForNode` / `nodeHardDependenciesSatisfied` that scans only `TaskGraph.Edges` where `EdgeType == EdgeHardDependency` and `To == nodeID`; unknown predecessor ids remain a gate/analyzer validation issue and should not panic the scheduler.
     2. Update `readyExplorerWindowContext` to skip non-optional nodes whose hard predecessors are not `nodeDone`, and keep optional nodes silent when their predecessors are not ready so routine status does not spam blocked optional nodes.
     3. Extend `nodeBlock` with dependency blocker ids or add a sibling typed blocker carrier so forced-finalize / render hints can distinguish entry-condition failures from dependency-wait state without parsing prose.
     4. Update scheduler unit tests from "merged initial chain" to "probe first, evidence after probe done, validate after evidence done"; add closure-status coverage so predecessor checks consume `EvidenceClosure.NodeExecStatus` over stale bootstrap maps.
     5. Update dispatch split tests that assumed all hard-dependent siblings were in the first window; preserve independent sibling parallelism only when their shared predecessor is already done.
     6. Run focused scheduler/dag tests before any broader read tests: `go test ./internal/orchestrator -run 'GraphState|ReadyWindow|Dependency|DagNode|AnalyzeRefine|SourceInventory|ExtractInputReady|IRDeliveryHotFileLineRatchet'`.
   - Delivered:
     1. `scheduler_dependency.go` centralizes hard-dependency blocker extraction and typed blocker rendering. `readyExplorerWindowContext` now gates pending/requeued explore-family nodes on `EdgeHardDependency` predecessors before evaluating entry conditions.
     2. Routine dependency waits stay silent while another ready node exists; blocker text only appears when the scheduler has no ready window, reducing repeated user-visible "waiting" noise.
     3. `scheduler_hard_stall.go` preserves hard-stall handoff by injecting inconclusive hypothesis verdicts before forced finalize.
     4. `stage_output_evidence_ingest.go` closes D1-G62 by routing StageOutput evidence into the same Mutable proof/reducer view used by CGEC, handoff, and finalizer paths.
     5. Checkpoint evidence counts now dedupe BusContext/output/Mutable/TurnA evidence views instead of summing parallel carriers.

     - `go test ./internal/orchestrator`

The first batch defines a typed readiness result with `ready/blocked/skip/reason_code/waiting_on/failed_criteria`, centralizes failed predecessor semantics, keeps optional dependency waits silent, preserves cancellation checks, and adds tests for failed predecessor, validation-feedback requeue, cancellation, optional silence, closure-status precedence, and current read E2E.

     2. Replace the duplicated checks inside `readyExplorerWindowContext` with `evaluateNodeReadinessContext(ctx,node,env)`. The ready-window renderer should convert only `Blocked` readiness into `nodeBlock`; skipped optional/status/stage nodes must stay silent.

For `MaxRetries > 0`, allowed dispatches are `1 + MaxRetries`; readiness blocks only a `nodeRequeued` node whose consumed attempts already reached that limit, so first pending dispatch is never blocked.

   - **Delivered D1-F10g.40 completion-form debt boundary（code complete / full regression passed）**:
     1. Target gaps: customer REPL logs showed a fully grounded investigation collecting 34 evidence items, then repeatedly retrying `emit_investigation_complete` for completion-form defects (`member_set.value="1+"`, decorated `member_set` labels missing `support_refs`, and aggregate handoff reshaping). After the operator skipped non-critical supplemental reads, the scheduler still reopened exploration and the model restarted `repo_map/read_file/emit_evidence`, so completion schema/form debt was misread as evidence-closure failure.
     2. Boundary principle: `completion schema/form failure != evidence closure failure`. Framework-owned completion-form debt must be repaired locally when safe, or carried as advisory/answer-side caveat when not answer-critical. It must not by itself reopen broad code exploration after an accepted/stable investigation closure.
     3. Implemented local normalization for exact `member_set` count metadata: `members[]` is the authoritative typed boundary, so non-integer/negative/drifted `value` surfaces are canonicalized to `len(members)` instead of forcing another model retry.
     4. Implemented local decorated-member form repair before the support-ref validator: ordinary resolved current-source members shaped like `CodeSymbol (qualifier)` are normalized to bare member identity plus aligned `member_notes`, with closed provenance `form_repair:decorated_member_base`. This preserves the answer content while avoiding downstream citation alignment failure and avoids asking the model to manually remove presentation qualifiers. The repair explicitly excludes absence lanes, VCS/runtime/command/external origin-specific lanes, and diagnostic lanes that require current-source verification; those still require explicit support refs or the existing origin-specific carrier.
     5. Added `RepairOriginCompletionFormPrefix` and classified framework-owned completion-form `RepairStructuredHandoff` as advisory/non-blocking for accepted closure. This keeps future completion-form repair descriptors from becoming new broad exploration blockers.
     6. What remains hard: illegal enums, mutually exclusive control fields, precise typed contradictions, and exact principal-member omissions that can change the answer set.      4. Decorated runtime/log/trace member-set rows can rely on typed runtime-artifact waiver/provenance when no current-source proof is required, preventing source `support_refs` form debt from becoming a false hard blocker.
     5. Local structured-handoff landing repair now exposes only `emit_investigation_complete`; `emit_evidence` is denied alongside navigation/search/trace/test/shell tools. Completion-form debt cannot reopen evidence collection.
     6. Red-line compliance: the fix consumes only structured waiver payload fields, closed waiver enums, typed runtime artifact context, typed current-source requirements, and dispatch-policy tool names. It does not inspect user text, model rationale, prompt text, rendered tool prose, localized REPL status, final-answer prose, wall time, or eval labels.
     7. Validation passed: `go test ./internal/tool -run 'TestEmitInvestigationComplete_(SameCallTraceWaiverBypassesDecoratedRuntimeSupportRefs|AllowsExhaustiveRuntimeArtifactMemberSet|DoesNotBypassMixedOriginExhaustiveMemberSet|Waiver)'`; `go test ./internal/orchestrator -run 'TestLocalLandingRepair|TestAcceptedClosure|TestShouldAutoCompleteExploreWindow'`; `go test ./internal/agent -run 'TestExplorer_(FilterToolSchemas_LocalLandingRepairPolicy|BuildInitialInstruction_LocalLandingRepairPolicy|RuntimeBoundary_LocalLandingRepairPolicy)'`; `go test ./internal/agent ./internal/orchestrator ./internal/tool`; `go test ./...`.
   - **Delivered D1-F10g.57 runtime current-source obligation precision boundary（code complete / full regression passed）**:
     1. Target gap: fresh customer HarmonyOS trace REPL showed `trace_query` had already produced answer-grade runtime evidence by the first investigation completion attempt, but `emit_investigation_complete` then treated `current_source_mode=allow` / source scope hints as a hard "must read current checkout source" requirement. The tool returned current-source repair, the scheduler reopened exploration, and the model was steered into `list_files` / `read_file` / repeat completion despite the user asking a trace-window causality question.
     2. Boundary principle: attached/typed runtime artifacts can be answer-authoritative by themselves. `allow current source` means source exploration is permitted, not mandatory. Only precise current-source obligations may hard-block completion: required `current_key_code` dimension, active current-source explanation / change-impact profile, exact resolution targets that are repo paths, explicit current-status diagnostic requirement, or a concrete current-source seed.
     3. `runtimeArtifactGroundingBypassAllowed` now uses `runtimeArtifactCurrentSourceHardRequirement` instead of the broad `RequiresCurrentSourceForExternalObservation` check. Runtime artifact context is derived from the bus (`AttachedLog` / `AttachedHitrace` / mutable runtime artifacts) and the `AnalysisIR.RequestModel` (`LogTriage` / `PerfTrace` / runtime artifact path reference), so completion gates no longer miss artifact-only traces that were represented only in typed request analysis.
     4. External-only runtime waivers are accepted when there is no precise current-source obligation; required current-source dimensions still block and produce a source-focused repair. This keeps trace/log causality answers from being forced into repository exploration while preserving the hard boundary for questions that explicitly require current implementation proof.
     5. Red-line compliance: routing consumes only typed request-model fields, answer-contract structs, runtime artifact attachment state, waiver enums, source path resolvers, and closed answer-dimension roles. It does not inspect user text, model rationale, prompt prose, rendered tool summaries, localized REPL status, final-answer prose, wall time, or eval labels.

     1. Target gap: the same trace REPL ended with the finalizer receiving `emit_answer_document`, but the dock still displayed `4/4 · 修复中：正在收集证据`. This was a renderer focus bug: an earlier explore/local-work repair stage could remain in `localStageKey` when live tool/content deltas omitted a stage field, so stale evidence-collection repair text survived into final answer generation.
     2. `bindLiveEventStageLocked` now falls back to the active typed task row when a live LLM/tool/progress event has no stage. This preserves the existing structural rule that status focus comes from typed event/task state, not from streamed prose or tool-call text.
     3. True post-finalizer backtracking still shows `修复中：...` when no live downstream row is active. Once the active finalizer row is receiving content or calling `emit_answer_document`, row 2 binds back to finalizer and no longer claims evidence collection is happening.

   - **Delivered D1-F10g.59 REPL auto-write route single-dispatch cutover（code complete / focused tests passed）**:
     1. Target gap: D1-G108. Structured TurnPolicy could correctly route a follow-up "please fix the above" request into write mode, but the REPL implemented that route by recursively calling `dispatchWithUserMode` after the current turn spinner had already started.
     2. Auto `RouteWrite` now enters a one-shot write mode in the current dispatch lifecycle via `enterOneShotUserMode`, sets `ModeApply` for the runner, and restores the previous user/pipeline mode after the run. Explicit `/write` and `/mode write` continue to use the same helper.
     3. Before auto-write lane switching, the current renderer spinner is stopped so write-disabled or unsettled-workflow gate messages cannot be hidden behind a stale live status row. The normal pipeline spinner restarts once the current dispatch reaches the runner.
     4. Red-line compliance: the route consumes only typed TurnPolicy fields (`route`, `write_intent`, access booleans, operation enum) and existing deterministic write-entry gates. It does not inspect user keywords, model rationale, prompt text, localized status text, final-answer prose, wall time, or eval labels.
     5. Validation passed: `go test ./internal/repl -run 'TestTurnPolicyDispatch_WriteRoute|TestEnterOneShotUserMode'`; `go test ./internal/repl ./internal/tool ./internal/orchestrator`.

     1. Target gap: D1-G109. Phase-1 pre-scan/ranker candidates can be stale or historical relative to the current checkout. Once the model has already attempted `read_file` for such a candidate and the current repository confirms it is absent/not a file, continuing to queue it as `phase1_unread` creates impossible pending debt.
     2. `raisePhase1UnreadPendingReads` now checks `forcedReadSeedUnavailableAfterAttempt` before queuing a pending read. The check requires a same-path failed `read_file` tool result plus an `os.Stat` confirmation against the current `RepoRoot`; without both precise signals, existing forced-read behavior is preserved.
     3. Unavailable candidates are skipped without burning the `phase1_unread` latch and without adding `PendingRead`, letting the answer path continue with caveat/audit instead of another broad exploration loop. Truly unread but available authority files still block as before.
     4. Red-line compliance: the fix consumes canonical repo-relative path keys, tool-name enums, failed `read_file` result history, and filesystem metadata. It does not inspect user wording, model rationale, prompt text, ranker prose, localized REPL status, final-answer prose, wall time, or eval labels.
